Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the oscillations space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 457 and the convergence rate is O(603/n^1).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to oscillations theory, we decompose the problem into 457 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 603. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
